Model before rewriting:

letting V be domain variant {a: bool, b: int(0, 4)}
find x: V
find y1: V
find y2: V
find z: V
find t1: V
find t2: V
find someInt: int(0)

such that

x[a] = true,
y1 = variant{a = false},
y2 = variant{b = 4},
x = z,
t1 = variant{b = someInt},
t2 = variant{b = t1[b]}

--

y1
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
y1
new variables:
  find y1#components_1: int(1..2)
  find y1#components_2: bool
  find y1#components_3: int(0, 4)
new constraints:
  (y1#components_1 != 1) -> (y1#components_2 = false)
  (y1#components_1 != 2) -> (y1#components_3 = 0)

--

y2
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
y2
new variables:
  find y2#components_1: int(1..2)
  find y2#components_2: bool
  find y2#components_3: int(0, 4)
new constraints:
  (y2#components_1 != 1) -> (y2#components_2 = false)
  (y2#components_1 != 2) -> (y2#components_3 = 0)

--

x
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x
new variables:
  find x#components_1: int(1..2)
  find x#components_2: bool
  find x#components_3: int(0, 4)
new constraints:
  (x#components_1 != 1) -> (x#components_2 = false)
  (x#components_1 != 2) -> (x#components_3 = 0)

--

x = z
   ~~> uniform_repr_in_comparison_op ([("ReprGeneral", 10100)])
x = z
new variables:

   ~~> variant_components_var_eq_var ([("ReprGeneral", 9700)])
